<file path="README.md">
<![CDATA[
# ASP VBScript CMS Administrative Modernization

This repository contains the `admin_crud` strangler-facade slice: a .NET 10 Web API, EF Core/SQL Server persistence, and a React TypeScript administrative SPA.

## Architecture

The React SPA calls same-origin `/api/v1/admin/*` endpoints through Axios with credentials. Authentication uses an opaque, server-side SQL Server session cookie plus a short-lived JWT in a separate HTTP-only cookie. State-changing requests require an in-memory CSRF token returned by the server. Page metadata, content revisions, and audit events are committed in one transaction.

## Prerequisites

- .NET 10 SDK
- SQL Server 2022 or later
- Node.js 20+ and npm

## Backend setup

```bash
dotnet restore
export ConnectionStrings__Default="Server=localhost;Database=CmsAdmin;Trusted_Connection=True;Encrypt=True;TrustServerCertificate=True"
export Admin__Jwt__Issuer="https://cms.example.test"
export Admin__Jwt__Audience="cms-admin"
export Admin__Jwt__Key="replace-with-a-long-random-secret"
export Admin__Seed__Username="admin"
dotnet ef migrations update --project backend
dotnet run --project backend
```

The seed password is not stored in source. Set `Admin__Seed__Password` to use a known password. If it is absent, the initializer generates one at startup and prints it to the console. The seeded administrator has `MustChangePassword=true`; the React login screen provides a reset flow.

## Frontend setup

```bash
cd frontend
npm install
npm run dev
```

The Vite development server proxies `/api` to `https://localhost:5001`. Production builds are served by the .NET application from `wwwroot`.

## Main routes

- Backend API: `/api/v1/admin`
- Swagger: `/swagger`
- Admin SPA: `/login`, `/`, `/pages`, `/pages/new`, `/pages/:id/edit`, `/maintenance`, `/unavailable`

The implementation deliberately does not copy legacy plaintext passwords, physical page files, legacy session cookies, or the legacy RC4 mechanism.
]]>
</file>
<file path="backend/backend.csproj">
<![CDATA[
<Project Sdk="Microsoft.NET.Sdk.Web">
  <PropertyGroup>
    <TargetFramework>net10.0</TargetFramework>
    <Nullable>enable</Nullable>
    <ImplicitUsings>enable</ImplicitUsings>
    <InvariantGlobalization>false</InvariantGlobalization>
    <GenerateDocumentationFile>true</GenerateDocumentationFile>
    <NoWarn>$(NoWarn);1591</NoWarn>
  </PropertyGroup>

  <ItemGroup>
    <PackageReference Include="AngleSharp" Version="1.3.0" />
    <PackageReference Include="FluentValidation.DependencyInjectionExtensions" Version="12.1.1" />
    <PackageReference Include="Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authentication.JwtBearer" Version="10.0.0" />
    <PackageReference Include="Microsoft.AspNetCore.OpenApi" Version="10.0.0" />
    <PackageReference Include="Microsoft.AspNetCore.RateLimiting" Version="10.0.0" />
    <PackageReference Include="Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Design" Version="10.0.0">
      <PrivateAssets>all</PrivateAssets>
      <IncludeAssets>runtime; build; native; contentfiles; analyzers; buildtransitive</IncludeAssets>
    </PackageReference>
    <PackageReference Include="Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.SqlServer" Version="10.0.0" />
    <PackageReference Include="Swashbuckle.AspNetCore" Version="7.2.0" />
  </ItemGroup>
</Project>
]]>
</file>

      "Audience": "${Admin:Jwt:Audience}",
      "Key": "${Admin:Jwt:Key}",
      "ExpiryMinutes": 60,

      "Default": "Information",
      "Microsoft.AspNetCore": "Warning",
      "Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Database.Command": "Information"

}

<file path="backend/Domain/Entities/UserRole.cs">
<![CDATA[
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations.Schema;

namespace AdminCrud.Domain.Entities;

public class UserRole
{
    public int Id { get; set; }

    [Required, StringLength(100)]
    public string Name { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [StringLength(500)]
    public string? Description { get; set; }

    public UserRoleLevel Level { get; set; }

    public ICollection<AdminUser> Users { get; set; } = new List<AdminUser>();
}
]]>

<file path="backend/Domain/Entities/AdminUser.cs">
<![CDATA[
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;
using AdminCrud.Domain.Enums;

namespace AdminCrud.Domain.Entities;

public class AdminUser
{
    public int Id { get; set; }

    [Required, StringLength(255)]
    public string Username { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [Required, StringLength(2048)]
    public string PasswordHash { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [Required, StringLength(255)]
    public string FullName { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [EmailAddress, StringLength(320)]
    public string? Email { get; set; }

    [Required]
    public int RoleId { get; set; }

    public bool MustChangePassword { get; set; }
    public bool IsActive { get; set; } = true;
    public DateTime? LastLoginAt { get; set; }

    [StringLength(500)]
    public string? Description { get; set; }

    [StringLength(100)]
    public string? FirstName { get; set; }

    [StringLength(100)]
    public string? SecondName { get; set; }

    [StringLength(255)]
    public string? Phone { get; set; }

    [StringLength(255)]
    public string? Address1 { get; set; }

    [StringLength(255)]
    public string? Address2 { get; set; }

    [StringLength(100)]
    public string? City { get; set; }

    [StringLength(100)]
    public string? State { get; set; }

    [StringLength(20)]
    public string? PostalCode { get; set; }

    [StringLength(100)]
    public string? Country { get; set; }

    public UserRole Role { get; set; } = null!;
}
]]>

<file path="backend/Domain/Entities/Page.cs">
<![CDATA[
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations.Schema;

namespace AdminCrud.Domain.Entities;

public class Page
{
    public int Id { get; set; }

    [Required, StringLength(120)]
    public string PageName { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [Required, StringLength(120)]
    public string PageTitle { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [Required, StringLength(500)]
    public string PageFileName { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    [StringLength(255)]
    public string? PageLinkHoverText { get; set; }

    [StringLength(250)]
    public string? PageDescription { get; set; }

    [StringLength(500)]
    public string? PageKeywords { get; set; }

    [Required, StringLength(100)]
    public string Style { get; set; } = "main";

    public int? MenuIndex { get; set; }
    public int? ParentPageId { get; set; }

    public bool MainMenu { get; set; }
    public bool Active { get; set; } = true;

    [Column(TypeName = "rowversion")]
    public byte[] Version { get; set; } = Array.Empty<byte>();

    public DateTime CreatedAt { get; set; } = DateTime.UtcNow;
    public DateTime UpdatedAt { get; set; } = DateTime.UtcNow;

    public Page? ParentPage { get; set; }
    public ICollection<Page> Children { get; set; } = new List<Page>();
    public ICollection<PageContent> Revisions { get; set; } = new List<PageContent>();
}
]]>

<file path="backend/Data/AppDbContext.cs">
<![CDATA[
using AdminCrud.Domain.Entities;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace AdminCrud.Data;

public sealed class AppDbContext : DbContext
{
    public AppDbContext(DbContextOptions<AppDbContext> options) : base(options)
    {
    }

    public DbSet<UserRole> Roles => Set<UserRole>();
    public DbSet<AdminUser> Users => Set<AdminUser>();
    public DbSet<Page> Pages => Set<Page>();
    public DbSet<PageContent> PageContents => Set<PageContent>();
    public DbSet<AuditLog> AuditLogs => Set<AuditLog>();
    public DbSet<AdminSession> AdminSessions => Set<AdminSession>();
    public DbSet<SiteSetting> SiteSettings => Set<SiteSetting>();

    protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder modelBuilder)
    {
        modelBuilder.ApplyConfigurationsFromAssembly(typeof(AppDbContext).Assembly);
        base.OnModelCreating(modelBuilder);
    }

    public override Task<int> SaveChangesAsync(CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        StampChanges();
        return base.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);
    }

    public override int SaveChanges()
    {
        StampChanges();
        return base.SaveChanges();
    }

    private void StampChanges()
    {
        var now = DateTime.UtcNow;
        foreach (var entry in ChangeTracker.Entries<IEntityWithTimestamps>())
        {
            if (entry.State == EntityState.Added)
            {
                entry.Entity.UpdatedAt = now;
            }
            else if (entry.State == EntityState.Modified)
            {
                entry.Entity.UpdatedAt = now;
            }
        }

        foreach (var entry in ChangeTracker.Entries<AuditLog>())
        {
            if (entry.State == EntityState.Added && entry.Entity.CreatedAt == default)
            {
                entry.Entity.CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;
            }
        }
    }
}

internal interface IEntityWithTimestamps
{
    DateTime UpdatedAt { get; set; }
}
]]>
</file>
<file path="backend/Data/Configurations/UserRoleConfiguration.cs">
<![CDATA[
using AdminCrud.Domain.Entities;
using AdminCrud.Domain.Enums;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Metadata.Builders;

namespace AdminCrud.Data.Configurations;

public sealed class UserRoleConfiguration : IEntityTypeConfiguration<UserRole>
{
    public void Configure(EntityTypeBuilder<UserRole> builder)
    {
        builder.ToTable("tblUserRoles");
        builder.HasKey(x => x.Id);
        builder.Property(x => x.Name).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(100);
        builder.Property(x => x.Description).HasMaxLength(500);
        builder.Property(x => x.Level).IsRequired();
        builder.HasIndex(x => x.Name).IsUnique();
        builder.HasIndex(x => x.Level).IsUnique();
        builder.HasData(
            new UserRole { Id = 1, Name = "Guest", Description = "Anonymous visitor", Level = UserRoleLevel.Guest },
            new UserRole { Id = 2, Name = "Registered", Description = "Registered site user", Level = UserRoleLevel.Registered },
            new UserRole { Id = 3, Name = "Editor", Description = "Can read and edit pages", Level = UserRoleLevel.Editor },
            new UserRole { Id = 4, Name = "Manager", Description = "Can manage pages and maintenance", Level = UserRoleLevel.Manager },
            new UserRole { Id = 5, Name = "Administrator", Description = "Full administrative access", Level = UserRoleLevel.Administrator });
    }
}
]]>
</file>
<file path="backend/Data/Configurations/AdminUserConfiguration.cs">
<![CDATA[
using AdminCrud.Domain.Entities;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Metadata.Builders;

namespace AdminCrud.Data.Configurations;

public sealed class AdminUserConfiguration : IEntityTypeConfiguration<AdminUser>
{
    public void Configure(EntityTypeBuilder<AdminUser> builder)
    {
        builder.ToTable("tblUsers");
        builder.HasKey(x => x.Id);
        builder.Property(x => x.Username).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(255);
        builder.Property(x => x.PasswordHash).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(2048);
        builder.Property(x => x.FullName).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(255);
        builder.Property(x => x.Email).HasMaxLength(320);
        builder.Property(x => x.Phone).HasMaxLength(255);
        builder.Property(x => x.Address1).HasMaxLength(255);
        builder.Property(x => x.Address2).HasMaxLength(255);
        builder.Property(x => x.City).HasMaxLength(100);
        builder.Property(x => x.State).HasMaxLength(100);
        builder.Property(x => x.PostalCode).HasMaxLength(20);
        builder.Property(x => x.Country).HasMaxLength(100);
        builder.HasIndex(x => x.Username).IsUnique();
        builder.HasIndex(x => x.RoleId);
        builder.HasOne(x => x.Role).WithMany(x => x.Users).HasForeignKey(x => x.RoleId).IsRequired().OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);
    }
}
]]>
</file>

<file path="backend/Data/SeedData/SeedData.cs">
<![CDATA[
using AdminCrud.Data;
using AdminCrud.Domain.Entities;
using AdminCrud.Domain.Enums;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Identity;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Configuration;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text;

namespace AdminCrud.Data.SeedData;

public static class SeedData
{
    public static async Task SeedAsync(IServiceProvider services, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var db = services.GetRequiredService<AppDbContext>();
        var configuration = services.GetRequiredService<IConfiguration>();
        var now = DateTime.UtcNow;

        await EnsureRoleAsync(db, "Guest", UserRoleLevel.Guest, cancellationToken);
        await EnsureRoleAsync(db, "Registered", UserRoleLevel.Registered, cancellationToken);
        await EnsureRoleAsync(db, "Editor", UserRoleLevel.Editor, cancellationToken);
        await EnsureRoleAsync(db, "Manager", UserRoleLevel.Manager, cancellationToken);
        await EnsureRoleAsync(db, "Administrator", UserRoleLevel.Administrator, cancellationToken);

        var adminRole = await db.Roles.SingleAsync(x => x.Level == UserRoleLevel.Administrator, cancellationToken);
        var username = configuration["Admin:Seed:Username"] ?? "admin";
        var seedPassword = configuration["Admin:Seed:Password"];
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(seedPassword))
        {
            seedPassword = GenerateStrongRandomPassword();
            Console.WriteLine($"[admin_crud] Generated seed administrator password: {seedPassword}");
        }

        var admin = await db.Users.SingleOrDefaultAsync(x => x.Username.Equals(username, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase), cancellationToken);
        if (admin is null)
        {
            admin = new AdminUser
            {
                Username = username,
                FullName = "Administrator",
                Role = adminRole,
                IsActive = true,
                MustChangePassword = true,
                PasswordHash = new PasswordHasher<AdminUser>().HashPassword(admin, seedPassword, PasswordHasherOptions.Default)
            };
            db.Users.Add(admin);
        }
        else
        {
            admin.Role = adminRole;
            admin.IsActive = true;
            admin.MustChangePassword = true;
        }

        if (!await db.Pages.AnyAsync(cancellationToken))
        {
            var home = new Page
            {
                PageName = "Home",
                PageTitle = "Home",
                PageFileName = "/home",
                Style = "main",
                Active = true,
                MainMenu = true,
                MenuIndex = 0,
                PageLinkHoverText = "Home",
                PageDescription = "Welcome to the site.",
                PageKeywords = "home, welcome",
                CreatedAt = now,
                UpdatedAt = now
            };
            db.Pages.Add(home);
            db.PageContents.Add(new PageContent
            {
                Page = home,
                Content = "<h1>Welcome</h1><p>This page was created by the administrative modernization seed.</p>",
                UpdatedAt = now
            });
        }

        await UpsertSettingAsync(db, "Maintenance", "Enabled", "False", "Boolean", "Whether the public site is offline.", 1, now, cancellationToken);
        await UpsertSettingAsync(db, "Maintenance", "Message", configuration["Maintenance:Message"] ?? "The system is down for scheduled maintenance. Please try back later.", "Text", null, 2, now, cancellationToken);

        await db.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);
    }

    private static async Task EnsureRoleAsync(AppDbContext db, string name, UserRoleLevel level, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var role = await db.Roles.SingleOrDefaultAsync(x => x.Name.Equals(name, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase), cancellationToken);
        if (role is null)
        {
            db.Roles.Add(new UserRole { Name = name, Level = level, Description = $"{name} administrator role" });
        }
        else
        {
            role.Level = level;
        }
    }

    private static async Task UpsertSettingAsync(AppDbContext db, string category, string name, string value, string valueType, string? description, int sortOrder, DateTime now, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var setting = await db.SiteSettings.SingleOrDefaultAsync(x => x.Category == category && x.Name == name, cancellationToken);
        if (setting is null)
        {
            db.SiteSettings.Add(new SiteSetting { Category = category, Name = name, Value = value, ValueType = valueType, Description = description, SortOrder = sortOrder, CreatedAt = now, UpdatedAt = now });
        }
        else
        {
            setting.Value = value;
            setting.ValueType = valueType;
            setting.Description = description;
            setting.SortOrder = sortOrder;
            setting.UpdatedAt = now;
        }
    }

    private static string GenerateStrongRandomPassword()
    {
        const string chars = "ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijkmnopqrstuvwxyz23456789!@#$%^&*()-_=+";
        var bytes = new byte[24];
        RandomNumberGenerator.Fill(bytes);
        var builder = new StringBuilder(24);
        foreach (var value in bytes) builder.Append(chars[value % chars.Length]);
        return builder.ToString();
    }
}
]]>
</file>
<file path="backend/Infrastructure/Persistence/DbInitializer.cs">
<![CDATA[
using AdminCrud.Data;
using AdminCrud.Data.SeedData;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace AdminCrud.Infrastructure.Persistence;

public static class DbInitializer
{
    public static async Task InitializeAsync(WebApplication app, CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        await using var db = app.Services.CreateAsyncScope().ServiceProvider.GetRequiredService<AppDbContext>();
        await db.Database.MigrateAsync(cancellationToken);
        await SeedData.SeedAsync(app.Services, cancellationToken);
    }
}

<file path="backend/Infrastructure/Auth/PasswordHasherService.cs">
<![CDATA[
using AdminCrud.Domain.Entities;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Identity;

namespace AdminCrud.Infrastructure.Auth;

public interface IPasswordHasherService
{
    Task<string> HashPasswordAsync(AdminUser user, string password);
    Task<PasswordVerificationResult> VerifyPasswordAsync(AdminUser user, string password);
    Task<IdentityResult> ValidatePasswordAsync(AdminUser user, string password);
}

public sealed class PasswordHasherService : IPasswordHasherService
{
    private readonly PasswordHasher<AdminUser> _hasher = new();

    public Task<string> HashPasswordAsync(AdminUser user, string password) =>
        Task.FromResult(_hasher.HashPassword(user, password, PasswordHasherOptions.Default));

    public Task<PasswordVerificationResult> VerifyPasswordAsync(AdminUser user, string password) =>
        Task.FromResult(_hasher.VerifyHashedPassword(user, user.PasswordHash, password));

    public Task<IdentityResult> ValidatePasswordAsync(AdminUser user, string password) =>
        Task.FromResult(new PasswordValidator<AdminUser>().ValidateAsync(null, user, password));
}
]]>

<file path="backend/Infrastructure/Auth/JwtTokenGenerator.cs">
<![CDATA[
using AdminCrud.Domain.Entities;
using System.IdentityModel.Tokens.Jwt;
using System.Security.Claims;
using System.Text;
using Microsoft.IdentityModel.Tokens;

namespace AdminCrud.Infrastructure.Auth;

public interface IJwtTokenGenerator
{
    string Generate(AdminUser user, DateTime expiresAt);
}

public sealed class JwtTokenGenerator : IJwtTokenGenerator
{
    private readonly JwtSettings _settings;

    public JwtTokenGenerator(IOptions<JwtSettings> settings)
    {
        _settings = settings.Value;
    }

    public string Generate(AdminUser user, DateTime expiresAt)
    {
        var key = new SymmetricSecurityKey(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(_settings.Key));
        var credentials = new SigningCredentials(key, SecurityAlgorithms.HmacSha256);
        var now = DateTime.UtcNow;
        var claims = new[]
        {
            new Claim(JwtRegisteredClaimNames.Sub, user.Id.ToString()),
            new Claim(JwtRegisteredClaimNames.Jti, Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N")),
            new Claim(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier, user.Id.ToString()),
            new Claim(ClaimTypes.Name, user.FullName),
            new Claim("username", user.Username),
            new Claim("role", user.Role.Name),
            new Claim("roleLevel", ((int)user.Role.Level).ToString()),
            new Claim(JwtRegisteredClaimNames.Iat, new DateTimeOffset(now).ToUnixTimeSeconds().ToString())
        };
        var token = new JwtSecurityToken(
            issuer: _settings.Issuer,
            audience: _settings.Audience,
            claims: claims,
            expires: expiresAt,
            signingCredentials: credentials);
        return new JwtSecurityTokenHandler().WriteToken(token);
    }
}
]]>
</file>
<file path="backend/Infrastructure/Middleware/ExceptionHandlingMiddleware.cs">
<![CDATA[
using AdminCrud.Contracts.Problems;
using AdminCrud.Services.Pages;
using FluentValidation;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Diagnostics;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace AdminCrud.Infrastructure.Middleware;

public sealed class ExceptionHandlingMiddleware
{
    private readonly RequestDelegate _next;
    private readonly IWebHostEnvironment _environment;

    public ExceptionHandlingMiddleware(RequestDelegate next, IWebHostEnvironment environment)
    {
        _next = next;
        _environment = environment;
    }

    public async Task InvokeAsync(HttpContext context)
    {
        var correlationId = context.Request.Headers["X-Correlation-Id"].FirstOrDefault()
            ?? context.Connection.TraceId
            ?? Activity.Current?.Id
            ?? Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");
        context.Response.Headers["X-Correlation-Id"] = correlationId;

        try
        {
            await _next(context);
        }
        catch (Exception exception)
        {
            if (context.Response.HasStarted) throw;
            var problem = MapException(exception, correlationId);
            context.Response.StatusCode = problem.Status;
            await context.Response.WriteAsJsonAsync(problem);
        }
    }

    private static ApiProblem MapException(Exception exception, string correlationId)
    {
        return exception switch
        {
            ValidationException validation => new ApiProblem(422, "VALIDATION_FAILED", "The request contains invalid values.", correlationId, validation.Errors.Select(x => new ValidationError(x.PropertyName, x.ErrorMessage)).ToList()),
            PageValidationException validation => new ApiProblem(422, "PAGE_VALIDATION_FAILED", validation.Message, correlationId, validation.Errors),
            PageNotFoundException => new ApiProblem(404, "NOT_FOUND", "The requested page was not found.", correlationId),
            PageConflictException conflict => new ApiProblem(409, "CONFLICT", conflict.Message, correlationId),
            PageVersionException version => new ApiProblem(412, "STALE_VERSION", version.Message, correlationId),
            MustChangePasswordException password => new ApiProblem(422, "MUST_CHANGE_PASSWORD", "Reset the administrator password before continuing.", correlationId),
            UnauthorizedException => new ApiProblem(401, "UNAUTHORIZED", "Authentication is required.", correlationId),
            DbUpdateException when exception.InnerException is System.Data.Common.DbException => new ApiProblem(500, "DATABASE_ERROR", "The database operation could not be completed.", correlationId),
            _ when _environment.IsDevelopment() => new ApiProblem(500, "INTERNAL_ERROR", exception.Message, correlationId),
            _ => new ApiProblem(500, "INTERNAL_ERROR", "An unexpected error occurred. Contact support with the correlation ID.", correlationId)
        };
    }
}
]]>
</file>
<file path="backend/Infrastructure/Middleware/JwtMiddleware.cs">
<![CDATA[
using AdminCrud.Infrastructure.Auth;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;

namespace AdminCrud.Infrastructure.Middleware;

public sealed class JwtMiddleware
{
    public const string TokenItemKey = "AdminCrud.JwtToken";
    private readonly RequestDelegate _next;

    public JwtMiddleware(RequestDelegate next) => _next = next;

    public async Task InvokeAsync(HttpContext context)
    {
        var token = context.Request.Headers.Authorization.ToString();
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(token)) token = context.Request.Cookies[AuthCookies.JwtCookieName];
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(token)) context.Items[TokenItemKey] = token;
        await _next(context);
    }
}
]]>
</file>

<file path="backend/Contracts/Problems/ApiProblem.cs">
<tool_call><![CDATA[
namespace AdminCrud.Contracts.Problems;

public sealed class ApiProblem
{
    public ApiProblem(int status, string code, string message, string correlationId, List<ValidationError>? details = null)
    {
        Status = status;
        Code = code;
        Message = message;
        CorrelationId = correlationId;
        Details = details ?? new();
    }

    public string Type { get; } = "about:blank";
    public string Title { get; } = "The request could not be completed.";
    public int Status { get; }
    public string Code { get; }
    public string Message { get; }
    public string CorrelationId { get; }
    public List<ValidationError> Details { get; }
}

public sealed class ValidationError
{
    public ValidationError(string field, string message)
    {
        Field = field;
        Message = message;
    }

    public string Field { get; }
    public string Message { get; }
}
]]>
</file>
</file>
</file>

<file path="backend/Contracts/Validators/CreatePageValidator.cs">
<tool_call><![CDATA[
using AdminCrud.Contracts.DTOs.Pages;
using FluentValidation;

namespace AdminCrud.Contracts.Validators;

public sealed class CreatePageValidator : AbstractValidator<CreatePageRequest>
{
    public CreatePageValidator()
    {
        RuleFor(x => x.PageName).NotEmpty().MaximumLength(120);
        RuleFor(x => x.PageTitle).NotEmpty().MaximumLength(120);
        RuleFor(x => x.PageContent).NotEmpty().MaximumLength(1_000_000);
        RuleFor(x => x.PageFileName).MaximumLength(500);
        RuleFor(x => x.PageLinkHoverText).MaximumLength(255);
        RuleFor(x => x.PageDescription).MaximumLength(250);
        RuleFor(x => x.PageKeywords).MaximumLength(500);
        RuleFor(x => x.Style).MaximumLength(100);
        RuleFor(x => x.MenuIndex).NullOrGreaterThan(0);
    }
}
]]>
